# 球队榜指标目录

每项输出 MD、HTML、JSON。阵容指标为画像赛季快照；缺失不补零。

| 指标目录 | 中文指标 |
|---|---|
| [averageAge](averageAge/averageAge.md) | 平均年龄 |
| [averageHeight](averageHeight/averageHeight.md) | 平均身高 |
| [averagePossession](averagePossession/averagePossession.md) | 平均控球率 |
| [awayStandings](awayStandings/awayStandings.md) | 客场积分榜 |
| [comebackPoints](comebackPoints/comebackPoints.md) | 落后追回分数 |
| [firstHalfStandings](firstHalfStandings/firstHalfStandings.md) | 上半场积分榜 |
| [homeStandings](homeStandings/homeStandings.md) | 主场积分榜 |
| [pointsDropped](pointsDropped/pointsDropped.md) | 领先丢掉分数 |
| [secondHalfStandings](secondHalfStandings/secondHalfStandings.md) | 下半场积分榜 |
| [shotConversionRate](shotConversionRate/shotConversionRate.md) | 射门转化率 |
| [shotOnTargetConversionRate](shotOnTargetConversionRate/shotOnTargetConversionRate.md) | 射正转化率 |
| [standings](standings/standings.md) | 积分榜 |
| [totalMarketValue](totalMarketValue/totalMarketValue.md) | 总身价 |
| [zeroShotsOnTarget](zeroShotsOnTarget/zeroShotsOnTarget.md) | 0射正 |
